HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_17c9d0f07ea155a230a7","text":"2. **Mutual consent after non-transactional giving.** Evidence: `ر ض و` B003 (mutual consent), activated by 92:19 no favor repaid and 92:20 face-seeking. Structural cue: after all human debts are excluded, a two-sided رضى remains. Mechanism: the purified giver and Lord meet in consent rather than exchange. Abductive move: I infer reciprocity without commerce. Reading change: the final promise is communion-like satisfaction, not wage-settlement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2b9da2c0fec139e1969c","text":"3. **Exploratory convergence rather than a one-way transaction.** `ر ض و:B003` supplies mutual consent, while `ر ض و:B004` preserves the causative possibility of making another satisfied. Seeking a face in 92:20 followed by unexpressed-subject `يَرْضَىٰ` leaves relational direction less explicit than the preceding material gift. The packet gives branch possibilities and sequence, but mutual convergence is an abductive extension, not a grammatical certainty. This final reading keeps satisfaction relational and potentially reciprocal while refusing to decide which party’s approval exhausts the ayah.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5b6007163c5fa7bb6606","text":"3. **Appeasement after warning.** Evidence: `ر ض و` B004 (making another satisfied/appeasing), activated by 92:14 warning and 92:17 avoidance of fire. Structural cue: terror is not the last word; رضى closes the sequence. Mechanism: the path from warning through giving removes displeasure. Abductive move: I infer that the warning’s purpose is to make this satisfaction possible. Reading change: the surah ends with resolved pleasure after alarm, not with fear alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_73cd600633f9f2f6292c","text":"No later ayah exists in the packet. Rereading the preceding portion of the clipped window added no later activation; `ر ض و:B002`, `ر ض و:B003`, and `ر ض و:B004` therefore remain explicitly relational and exploratory beside the primary satisfaction branch `ر ض و:B001`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001","root_000569/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8807548af97760f705cb","text":"2. **The satisfaction sought and the satisfaction attained remain coactive.** `ر ض و:B002` names abundant or specially sought approval, and the immediately preceding `ٱبْتِغَآءَ` activates its “sought” scope even though the fixed active verb primarily carries `ر ض و:B001`. The abductive move is to let the sought object in 92:20 specify the relational field of the later satisfaction without changing the verb’s morphology. The ayah can therefore hold both the agent’s becoming satisfied and an orientation toward approval, rather than forcing one to erase the other.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9d122faf488cf897a6c6","text":"1. **Future satisfaction as accepted outcome.** Evidence: `ر ض و` B001 (acceptance opposed to displeasure), B002 (sought/abundant approval). Structural cue: the future particle follows purified seeking of the Lord’s face. Mechanism: the sought face answers with satisfaction, whether God’s approval, the giver’s contentment, or both are activated. Abductive move: I preserve both directions because the root allows approval as state and sought approval as object. Reading change: the ending is relational acceptance, not merely payment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a03a7e38027cf81565a9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:21:2\",\"root_000569\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gelecekteki hoşnutluk, arınmış ve tek yöne çevrilmiş vermenin eksilmeyle değil kabul ve doyumla sonuçlanan gecikmiş varış noktasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a6662da280f57df1e6f2","text":"1. **Satisfaction as the delayed endpoint of the giving course.** `يَرْضَىٰ` directly anchors `ر ض و:B001` (acceptance and satisfaction opposed to displeasure), and `لَسَوْفَ` makes that state prospective. The carried-forward agent gives and purifies himself in 92:18, has no human repayment claim in 92:19, and seeks a single direction in 92:20. The packet supplies the sequence; the inferred arrow is that purified, directed giving terminates in acceptance rather than depletion. Satisfaction consequently reads as the course’s eventual state, not a disconnected promise of pleasant feeling.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_afba6a4ecda60946b565","text":"92:20’s “Highest” retrospectively turned this fall into a vertical contrast: wealth pulls downward while the sought face belongs above. 92:19 also clarified that the failed مال is not just insufficient currency but a bad substitute in an economy where only God-facing giving works.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c291700f4ecd451d4463","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:21:2\",\"root_000569\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Önceki arayış, kişinin hoşnut olmasıyla aranan hoşnutluğun aynı ilişkisel alanda birlikte etkin kalmasına imkân verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003","root_000569/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f005503377928ed8d281","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:21:2\",\"root_000569\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Karşılıksız vermenin ardından hoşnutluk, ticari olmayan karşılıklı bir kabul veya birini hoşnut etme yakınlaşması olarak okunabilir; öznenin yönü bunu kesinleştirmez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
